Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any composites module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 45. The projective resolution has length at most 993 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the composites space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 45 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 993. QED.
